			end
		end

			instance.Parent = parent
		end

	end

local v

local function fn()
	if not modules then
		return nil
	end
	local network = modules:FindFirstChild("Network") or modules:WaitForChild("Network", 10)
	if not network then
		local network2 = modules:FindFirstChild("Network", true)
		return network2 and network2:IsA("ModuleScript") and network2 or nil
	end

	if network:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return network
	end
	local network2 = network:FindFirstChild("Network") or network:WaitForChild("Network", 10)
	if network2 and network2:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return network2
	end

	for _, descendant in ipairs(modules:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant.Name == "Network" and descendant:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			return descendant
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local fn11
local tbl5 = { Track = nil, AnimObj = nil }

fn11 = function()
	local character = localPlayer.Character
	if not character then
		return
	end
	local tbl6 = {}
	local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	local upperTorso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	local lowerTorso = character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
	local findFirstChild = character.FindFirstChild
	local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	tbl6[1] = torso
	tbl6[2] = upperTorso
	tbl6[3] = lowerTorso
	tbl6[4] = humanoidRootPart

	do
		local values = table.pack(findFirstChild(character, "Head"))
		table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, tbl6)
	end

	if not tbl.Invisibility then
		for _, v5 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if v5 then
				v5.CanCollide = true
			end
		end

		if tbl5.Track then
			tbl5.Track:Stop()
			tbl5.Track = nil
		end
	else
		for _, v5 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if v5 then
				v5.CanCollide = false
			end
		end

		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		humanoid = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

		if humanoid then
			if not tbl5.AnimObj then
				tbl5.AnimObj = Instance.new("Animation")
				tbl5.AnimObj.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://75804462760596"
			end

			if tbl5.Track then
				tbl5.Track:Stop()
			end

			tbl5.Track = humanoid:LoadAnimation(tbl5.AnimObj)
			tbl5.Track:Play()
			tbl5.Track:AdjustSpeed(0)
		end
	end
end

local fn12

fn12 = function(arg)
	local originPos = arg.OriginPos
	local searchType = arg.SearchType or "World_3D"
	local targetFolder = arg.TargetFolder
	local maxDistance = arg.MaxDistance or math.huge
	local fovRadius = arg.FOVRadius or math.huge
	local wallCheck = arg.WallCheck or false
	local ignoreList = arg.IgnoreList or {}
	if not targetFolder or not originPos then
		return nil, nil
	end
	local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local children

	if targetFolder == killers then
		children = damnedFK.Roster.killers()
	elseif targetFolder == survivors then
		children = damnedFK.Roster.survivors()
	else
		children = targetFolder:GetChildren()
	end

	local huge = math.huge
	local v5 = nil
	local v6 = nil

	for _, child in ipairs(children) do
		local humanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

		if humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
			local flag = false

			for _, v7 in ipairs(ignoreList) do
				if child == v7 then
					flag = true
					break
				end
			end

			if not flag then
				local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - currentCamera2.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

				if searchType == "World_3D" then
					magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - originPos).Magnitude
				end

				if magnitude <= maxDistance then
					if wallCheck then
						local v7 = damnedFK.rayParams(ignoreList)
						local position = currentCamera2.CFrame.Position
						local hit = workspace:Raycast(position, humanoidRootPart.Position - position, v7)

						if not (hit and not hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(child)) then
							if searchType == "World_3D" then
								if magnitude < huge then
									huge = magnitude
									v5 = child
									v6 = humanoidRootPart
								end
							elseif searchType == "Screen_2D" then
								local v8, v9 = currentCamera2:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart.Position)

								if v9 then
									local magnitude2 = (Vector2.new(v8.X, v8.Y) - originPos).Magnitude

									if magnitude2 <= fovRadius and magnitude2 < huge then
										huge = magnitude2
										v5 = child
										v6 = humanoidRootPart
									end
								end
							end
						end
					elseif searchType == "World_3D" then
						if magnitude < huge then
							huge = magnitude
							v5 = child
							v6 = humanoidRootPart
						end
					elseif searchType == "Screen_2D" then
						local v7, v8 = currentCamera2:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart.Position)

						if v8 then
							local magnitude2 = (Vector2.new(v7.X, v7.Y) - originPos).Magnitude

							if magnitude2 <= fovRadius and magnitude2 < huge then
								huge = magnitude2
								v5 = child
								v6 = humanoidRootPart
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return v5, v6
end

	local vector2 = arg.Screen2D and Vector2.new(currentCamera.ViewportSize.X / 2, currentCamera.ViewportSize.Y / 2) or v5 and v5.Position

		SearchType = arg.Screen2D and "Screen_2D" or "World_3D",

		FOVRadius = arg.FOV or math.huge,
		WallCheck = arg.WallCheck or false,
		IgnoreList = arg.IgnoreSelf and { localPlayer.Character } or {},
	})
end

Visual = v2:AddTab("Visual")

createHighlight = function(adornee, fillColor, arg)
	if not adornee or not adornee.Parent then
		return
	end

	if arg and arg.CheckWorkspace then
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return adornee:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
		end)

		if not ok or not result then
			return
		end
	end

	local highlight = adornee:FindFirstChild("Highlight")

	if highlight then
		highlight:Destroy()
	end

	local highlight2 = Instance.new("Highlight")
	highlight2.Adornee = adornee
	highlight2.FillColor = fillColor
	highlight2.OutlineColor = fillColor
	highlight2.FillTransparency = arg and arg.FillTransparency or tbl.HFillTransparency or 0.8
	highlight2.OutlineTransparency = arg and arg.OutlineTransparency or tbl.DisableOutline or 0
	highlight2.Parent = adornee
	return highlight2
end

do
	local name = "External_Protected_HL"
	local tbl6 = {}

	createHighlight2 = function(adornee, fillColor)
		if not adornee or not adornee.Parent then
			return
		end
		local name2 = localPlayer.Name
		if adornee:GetAttribute("Username") == name2 then
			return
		end

		if tbl6[adornee] then
			local externalProtectedHl = adornee:FindFirstChild("External_Protected_HL")

			if externalProtectedHl then
				externalProtectedHl.FillColor = fillColor
				externalProtectedHl.OutlineColor = fillColor
				return
			end
		end

		local function fn14()
			for _, descendant in ipairs(adornee:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Highlight") and descendant.Name ~= name then
					descendant:Destroy()
					break
				end
			end

			local externalProtectedHl = adornee:FindFirstChild("External_Protected_HL")

			if not externalProtectedHl then
				externalProtectedHl = Instance.new("Highlight")
				externalProtectedHl.Name = name
				externalProtectedHl.Adornee = adornee
				externalProtectedHl.Parent = adornee
			end

			externalProtectedHl.FillColor = fillColor
			externalProtectedHl.OutlineColor = fillColor
			externalProtectedHl.FillTransparency = tbl.HFillTransparency or 0.5
			externalProtectedHl.OutlineTransparency = tbl.DisableOutline or 0
			externalProtectedHl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		end

		fn14()

		tbl6[adornee] = adornee.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Highlight") and child.Name ~= name then
				task.wait(3)

				task.defer(function()
					if child.Parent then
						child:Destroy()
					end

					fn14()
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

					createHighlight2(child, tbl.EspKillerSupportColor)

					for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do

						end
					end

					local trailESPModel = child:FindFirstChild("TrailESPModel")

						trailESPModel.Parent = child
					end

					for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
						if child2:IsA("BasePart") then
							child2.Transparency = 0
							child2.Parent = trailESPModel
						end
					end

					createHighlight2(trailESPModel, tbl.EspKillerColor)
				end
			end

			connection2 = v5.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				task.wait(0.5)
				createHighlight2(child, tbl.EspKillerColor)
			end)

			connection3 = ingame.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				task.wait(0.5)
				local humanoid = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) and humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= 16 and humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= 26 then
					createHighlight2(child, tbl.EspKillerSupportColor)
				elseif child:IsA("Folder") and string.sub(child.Name, -7) == "Shadows" then

					end)

					local model = Instance.new("Model")
					model.Name = "TrailESPModel"
					model.Parent = child

					for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do

							child2.Transparency = 0
							child2.Parent = model
						end
					end

					createHighlight2(model, tbl.EspKillerColor)
				end
			end)
		else
			for _, child in ipairs(killers:GetChildren()) do
				if tbl6[child] then
					tbl6[child]:Disconnect()
					tbl6[child] = nil
				end

				local externalProtectedHl = child:FindFirstChild("External_Protected_HL") or child:FindFirstChild("Highlight")

				if externalProtectedHl then
					externalProtectedHl:Destroy()
				end
			end

				if descendant.Name == name then
					descendant:Destroy()
				end

				if descendant.Name == "Shadow" then
					descendant.Transparency = 1
				end
			end

					local trailESPModel = child:FindFirstChild("TrailESPModel")

								child2.Transparency = 1
								child2.Parent = child
							end
						end

					end

						if child2:IsA("BasePart") then

						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

			{ Name = "Shoot", Anim = "AimGun", CooldownKey = "ShootCooldown" },

	local tbl13 = { "rbxassetid://99218231350350", "rbxassetid://115049033276951", "rbxassetid://70948225384237" }
	local tbl14 = { BloxyCola = 10, SlateskinPotion = 6, Ghostburger = 10 }

	fn14 = function(arg)
		if not assets then
			return nil
		end
		local v6 = assets

		for _, v7 in ipairs(arg) do
			if v6:FindFirstChild(v7) then
				v6 = v6[v7]
				continue
			end
			return nil
		end

		if v6:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			local ok, result = pcall(require, v6)
			if ok and type(result) == "table" then
				return result
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn16(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg then
			return {}
		end
		local tbl15

		if type(arg2) == "string" then
			tbl15 = { arg2 }
		else
			tbl15 = arg2
		end

		local tbl16 = {}

		local function fn17(arg4)
			if type(arg4) == "string" or type(arg4) == "number" then
				return string.match(tostring(arg4), "%d+")
			end
			return nil
		end

		if arg3 and arg[arg3] then
			local v6 = fn17(arg[arg3])

			if v6 then
				table.insert(tbl16, v6)
			end
		end

		if tbl15 then
			for _, v6 in ipairs(tbl15) do
				if arg[v6] then
					local v7 = fn17(arg[v6])

					if v7 then
						table.insert(tbl16, v7)
					end
				end

				local v7 = string.split(v6, ".")
				local v8 = arg

				for _, v9 in ipairs(v7) do
					if type(v8) == "table" then
						v8 = v8[v9]
					else
						v8 = nil
						break
					end
				end

				local v9 = fn17(v8)

				if v9 then
					table.insert(tbl16, v9)
				end

				if #v7 > 1 then
					local v10 = v7[#v7]

					if arg[v10] then
						local v11 = fn17(arg[v10])

						if v11 then
							table.insert(tbl16, v11)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if #tbl16 == 0 then
			local fn18 = nil

			fn18 = function(arg4)
				for k, v6 in pairs(arg4) do
					if type(v6) == "table" then
						fn18(v6)
					else
						local v7 = fn17(v6)

						if v7 then
							local str2 = tostring(k):lower()
							local pos = arg3 and str2:find(arg3:lower(), 1, true)
							local flag2 = false

							if pos then
								flag2 = true
							end

							if tbl15 then
								for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl15) do
									if str2:find(v8:lower(), 1, true) or v8:lower():find(str2, 1, true) then
										flag2 = true
										break
									end
								end
							end

							if flag2 then
								table.insert(tbl16, v7)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			pcall(fn18, arg)
		end

		return tbl16
	end

	local function fn17(arg)
		local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("CharacterName") or arg.Name
		local attribute2 = arg:GetAttribute("SkinName") or ""
		local str2 = arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Name == "Killers" and "Killers" or "Survivors"
		local v6 = fn14({ str2, attribute, "Config" })
		local v7 = fn14({ str2, attribute, "Behavior" })
		local flag2 = attribute2 ~= "" and fn14({ "Skins", str2, attribute, attribute2, "Config" }) or nil
		local tbl15 = {}

		if v7 and v7.Abilities then
			local v8 = tbl12[attribute]

			if v8 then
				for i, v9 in ipairs(v8) do
					local name = v9.Name
					local v10 = v7.Abilities[name]

					if v10 then
						local icon = v10.Icon or ""

						if name == "Broadcast" and attribute == "Veeronica" then
							icon = tbl13[1]
						end

						local tbl16 = {}
						local cooldown

						if typeof(v10.Cooldown) == "number" then
							cooldown = v10.Cooldown
						elseif v9.CooldownKey and v6 then
							cooldown = v6[v9.CooldownKey]
						else
							cooldown = nil

							if v6 then
								cooldown = v6[name .. "Cooldown"]
							end
						end

						if v9.Anim then
							local anim = v9.Anim

							if flag2 and flag2.Animations then
								tbl16 = fn16(flag2.Animations, anim, name)
							end

							if #tbl16 == 0 and v6 and v6.Animations then
								tbl16 = fn16(v6.Animations, anim, name)
							end
						end

						local activeCondition

						if attribute == "Slasher" and name == "RagingPace" then
							activeCondition = "IsEnraged"
						elseif attribute == "Veeronica" and name == "Sk8" then
							activeCondition = "IsSkating"
						elseif attribute == "Noob" and tbl14[name] then
							activeCondition = name .. "Active"
						else
							local flag3 = attribute == "TwoTime" and name == "Crouch"
							activeCondition = nil

							if flag3 then
								activeCondition = "IsCrouching"
							end
						end

						if #tbl16 > 0 or v9.CustomTrigger then
							local tbl17 = { AnimIds = tbl16 }
							cooldown = cooldown or 0
							tbl17.Cooldown = cooldown
							tbl17.Icon = icon
							tbl17.AlwaysShow = v9.AlwaysShow or false
							tbl17.CycleIcons = v9.CycleIcons or false
							tbl17.CustomTrigger = v9.CustomTrigger
							tbl17.LayoutOrder = i
							tbl17.ActiveCondition = activeCondition
							tbl15[name] = tbl17
						end
					end
				end
			else
				local n5 = 0

				for k, ability in pairs(v7.Abilities) do
					n5 += 1
					local layoutOrder = ability.LayoutOrder or n5
					local icon = ability.Icon or ""
					local cooldown = typeof(ability.Cooldown) == "number" and ability.Cooldown or v6 and v6[k .. "Cooldown"] or 0
					local tbl16 = {}

					if flag2 and flag2.Animations then
						tbl16 = fn16(flag2.Animations, nil, k)
					end

					if #tbl16 == 0 and v6 and v6.Animations then
						tbl16 = fn16(v6.Animations, nil, k)
					end

					if #tbl16 > 0 then
						tbl15[k] = {
							AnimIds = tbl16,
							Cooldown = cooldown,
							Icon = icon,
							AlwaysShow = false,
							CycleIcons = false,
							LayoutOrder = layoutOrder,
							ActiveCondition = nil,
						}
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if next(tbl15) == nil and v6 and v6.Animations then
			local n5 = 0

			for k in pairs(v6.Animations) do
				local v8 = v6[k .. "Cooldown"]

				if v8 then
					local tbl16 = {}

					if flag2 and flag2.Animations then
						tbl16 = fn16(flag2.Animations, nil, k)
					end

					if #tbl16 == 0 then
						tbl16 = fn16(v6.Animations, nil, k)
					end

					if #tbl16 > 0 then
						n5 += 1

						tbl15[k] = {
							AnimIds = tbl16,
							Cooldown = v8,
							Icon = "",
							AlwaysShow = false,
							CycleIcons = false,
							LayoutOrder = n5,
							ActiveCondition = nil,
						}
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return tbl15
	end

	local function fn18(arg, arg2)
		if arg:GetAttribute("FootstepOverride") == "GolemFootsteps" then
			return true
		end

		if arg2 and arg2.AbilityState and arg2.AbilityState.Animator then
			for _, v6 in ipairs(arg2.AbilityState.Animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
				if v6.Animation and v6.Animation.AnimationId then
					if tostring(v6.Animation.AnimationId):lower():find("golem") then
						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn19(arg, arg2)
		if fn18(arg, arg2) then
			return { MaxStamina = 50, StaminaGain = 20, StaminaLoss = 10, MinStamina = 0, PenaltyTime = 2 }
		end
		local str2 = arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Name == "Survivors" and "Survivors" or "Killers"
		local name = arg.Name
		if str2 == "Survivors" then
			return tbl10
		end
		local v6 = fn14({ "Killers", name, "Config" })

		if v6 then
			return {
				MaxStamina = v6.MaxStamina or 100,
				StaminaGain = v6.StaminaGain or 20,
				StaminaLoss = v6.StaminaLoss or 10,
				MinStamina = 0,
				PenaltyTime = 2,
			}
		end

		return tbl10
	end

	local function fn20(arg, arg2, arg3)

			if v6 and v6.Animations and v6.Animations[str3] then
				return v6.Animations[str3]

		local v6 = fn14({ str2, name, "Config" })

			return v6.Animations[str3]

		if arg3 then
			return "rbxassetid://130480436367274"

			if v7 and v7.Animations and v7.Animations.Run then
				return v7.Animations.Run
			end
		end

		return arg2 and "rbxassetid://115946474977409" or "rbxassetid://136252471123500"
	end

	local function fn21(arg, arg2)

			return false

				local v7 = string.match(tostring(v6.Animation.AnimationId), "%d+")
				if arg2 and v7 == arg2 and v6.WeightCurrent > 0.45 then
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn22(arg)
		if arg and arg.MaxHealth > 0 then
			return arg.Health / arg.MaxHealth
		end
		return 1
	end

				if humanoidRootPart and (humanoidRootPart.Position - arg).Magnitude <= n4 then

	end

	local function fn25(arg)
		local now = os.clock()
		if arg.LastCycleTime and now - arg.LastCycleTime < 0.5 then
			return
		end
		arg.LastCycleTime = now
		arg.BroadcastChannelIndex = arg.BroadcastChannelIndex % 3 + 1
		local v6 = tbl13[arg.BroadcastChannelIndex]

			if broadcast then
				local imageLabel = broadcast:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel", true)

				if imageLabel and imageLabel:IsA("ImageLabel") then
					imageLabel.Image = v6
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn27(arg, arg2)
		if not arg2.Animation or not arg2.Animation.AnimationId then
			return
		end
		local v6 = string.match(tostring(arg2.Animation.AnimationId), "%d+")
		if not v6 then
			return
		end

		for k, ability in pairs(arg.Abilities) do
			if ability.AnimIds and #ability.AnimIds > 0 and fn24(ability, v6) then
				if not arg.Timers[k] or arg.Timers[k] <= 0 then
					arg.Timers[k] = ability.Cooldown

					if ability.CycleIcons then
						fn25(arg)
					end

					local playerStateRef = arg.PlayerStateRef

					if playerStateRef and playerStateRef.NoobEffects and ability.ActiveCondition and tbl14[k] then
						local v7 = tbl14[k]
						playerStateRef.NoobEffects[k] = tick() + v7
						playerStateRef[ability.ActiveCondition] = true
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if arg.EnragedAnimIds and arg.EnragedAnimIds[v6] then
			local playerStateRef = arg.PlayerStateRef

			if playerStateRef then
				playerStateRef.IsEnraged = true
				playerStateRef.EnrageStartTime = tick()
			end
		end

		if arg.Sk8StartAnimId and v6 == arg.Sk8StartAnimId then
			local playerStateRef = arg.PlayerStateRef

			if playerStateRef then
				playerStateRef.IsSkating = true
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn28(arg, arg2, arg3, hum)
		local v6 = fn18(arg, nil)
		local v7 = fn19(arg, nil)
		local animator = hum and hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
		local str2 = arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Name == "Survivors" and "Survivor" or "Killer"

		local isInjured = v8 <= 0.5

		local v10 = fn20(arg, true, false)
		local v11 = fn20(arg, false, true)

		local v13 = string.match(tostring(v10 or ""), "%d+")
		local v14 = string.match(tostring(v11 or ""), "%d+")

		tbl15.InjuredRunAnimationIdStr = v13
		tbl15.GolemRunAnimationIdStr = v14
		tbl15.IsInjured = isInjured
		tbl15.LastHealthCheck = v8
		tbl15.AbilityState = nil
		tbl15.Hum = hum
		tbl15.IsStaminaFrozen = false
		tbl15.IsEnraged = false
		tbl15.EnragedAnimIds = nil
		tbl15.EnrageStartTime = 0
		tbl15.IsSkating = false
		tbl15.Sk8SkatingAnimId = nil
		tbl15.IsCrouching = false
		tbl15.NoobEffects = {}
		tbl15.BloxyColaActive = false
		tbl15.SlateskinPotionActive = false
		tbl15.GhostburgerActive = false
		tbl15.CrouchAttrListener = nil
		tbl15.IsGolem = v6
		tbl15.GolemGraceUntil = v6 and tick() + 3 or 0
		local abilityState = nil

		if arg2 and animator then

			if v15 then
				abilityState = {

					TimersUI = {},
					IconLabels = {},

				for k, v16 in pairs(v15) do
					if v16.AlwaysShow then
						createFrame(abilityState, k, v16)
					end
				end

				table.insert(abilityState.Connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg4)
					fn27(abilityState, arg4)
				end))

				for _, v16 in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					fn27(abilityState, v16)
				end

				if arg.Name == "Slasher" then
					local enragedAnimIds = {}
					local v16 = fn14({ "Killers", "Slasher", "Config" })

					if v16 and v16.Animations then
						for _, v17 in ipairs({
							"EnragedStart",
							"EnragedIdle",
							"EnragedWalk",
							"EnragedRun",
							"EnragedSlash",
							"EnragedBehead",
						}) do
							local v18 = v16.Animations[v17]

							if v18 then
								local v19 = string.match(tostring(v18), "%d+")

								if v19 then
									enragedAnimIds[v19] = true
								end
							end
						end
					end

					abilityState.EnragedAnimIds = enragedAnimIds
				end

				if arg.Name == "Veeronica" then
					local v16 = fn14({ "Survivors", "Veeronica", "Config" })

					if v16 and v16.Animations then
						local sk8Skating = v16.Animations.Sk8Skating
						local sk8Start = v16.Animations.Sk8Start

						if sk8Skating then
							tbl15.Sk8SkatingAnimId = string.match(tostring(sk8Skating), "%d+")

						end

						if sk8Start then
							abilityState.Sk8StartAnimId = string.match(tostring(sk8Start), "%d+")
						end
					end
				end

				if arg.Name == "TwoTime" then
					local function fn29()
						tbl15.IsCrouching = arg:GetAttribute("Crouching") == true
					end

					fn29()
					tbl15.CrouchAttrListener = arg:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Crouching"):Connect(fn29)
				end
			end
		end

		tbl15.AbilityState = abilityState
		tbl8[arg] = tbl15
	end

	local function fn29(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg or not arg.Parent then
			return
		end

		Name = "Projectile ESP",
		Callback = function(arg)
			tbl8.SetEnabled(arg)

			if arg then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return require(ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player.Game.ProjectileReplication)
				end)

				local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
					return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Gameplay.PredictProjectile)
				end)

				if not (ok and ok2 and result and result2) then
					return
				end

				if v9 then
					v9:Disconnect()
				end

				if type(fn27) == "function" then
					fn27()
				end

				local max = math.max
				local vector2 = Vector3.new
				local cframe = CFrame.new
				local cframe2 = CFrame.lookAt

				local function fn28(arg2)
					local model = arg2.Model

					if model then
						if model:IsA("Model") then
							local extentsSize = model:GetExtentsSize()
							return max(extentsSize.X, extentsSize.Y)
						end

						if model:IsA("BasePart") then
							return max(model.Size.X, model.Size.Y)
						end
					end

					local n6 = 0.8

					if not n4 then
						n6 = 1
					end

					return n6
				end

				local function fn29(arg2)
					local position = arg2.InitialCFrame.Position
					local initialVelocity = arg2.InitialVelocity
					local gravity = arg2.Gravity or 0
					local v10 = fn28(arg2)
					local n6 = result2:FindLeadShot(position, initialVelocity, initialVelocity.Magnitude, position, Vector3.zero, gravity)

					if not n6 or n6 == position then
						local n7 = 3

						if not n3 then
							n7 = 1
						end

						n6 = position + initialVelocity * n7 + vector2(0, gravity * 0.5 * n7 * n7, 0)
					end

					local magnitude = (n6 - position).Magnitude
					local part = Instance.new("Part")
					part.Name = "ESP_Line_" .. tostring(arg2.UUID or "Unknown")
					part.Anchored = true
					part.CanCollide = false
					part.CanQuery = false
					part.CanTouch = false
					part.CastShadow = false
					part.Size = vector2(v10, v10, magnitude)
					part.CFrame = cframe2(position, n6) * cframe(0, 0, -magnitude * 0.5)
					part.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
					part.Color = color2 or Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
					part.Transparency = 0.5
					part.Parent = workspace
					local boxHandleAdornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
					boxHandleAdornment.Size = part.Size
					boxHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
					boxHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 10
					boxHandleAdornment.Color3 = color2 or Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
					boxHandleAdornment.Transparency = 0.4
					boxHandleAdornment.Adornee = part
					boxHandleAdornment.CFrame = cframe()
					boxHandleAdornment.Parent = part
					return part, boxHandleAdornment, n6, v10
				end

				local heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat
				local connect = heartbeat.Connect
				local v10 = (function(Q) return function(f)local C=Q[1].Projectiles;if not C then return;end;for m,H in Q[2](C)do if Q[3][m]==nil and H.InitialCFrame and H.InitialVelocity then local d,E,L,a=Q[4][6][Q[4][3]](H);Q[3][m]={Part=d,Highlight=E,Age=0,StartPos=H.InitialCFrame.Position,Velocity=H.InitialVelocity,Gravity=H.Gravity or 0,EndPos=L,Thickness=a};end;end;for m,H in Q[2](Q[3])do if Q[5](H)=="table"then local d=H.Age+f;H.Age=d;local f=C[m];local E=H.Part;if not f or f.Running==false then if E then E:Destroy();end;Q[3][m]=nil;elseif d>=(Q[6]or 10)then if E then E:Destroy();end;Q[3][m]="Expired";else local L=f.Model;local f=if L and L.Parent then L:GetPivot().Position else H.StartPos+H.Velocity*d+Q[7](0,H.Gravity*0.5*(d*d),0);if E and E.Parent then L=H.EndPos-f;local d,a=L.Magnitude,H.EndPos-H.StartPos;if(a.Magnitude>1.0E-4 and a.Unit or(Vector3.new(0.0,0.0,0.0))):Dot(L.Magnitude>1.0E-4 and L.Unit or(Vector3.new(0.0,0.0,0.0)))>0 and d>0.5 then local L=Q[7](H.Thickness,H.Thickness,d);E.Size=L;E.CFrame=Q[8](f,H.EndPos)*Q[9](0,0,-d*0.5);if H.Highlight then H.Highlight.Size=L;end;else E:Destroy();Q[3][m]="Expired";end;end;end;elseif H=="Expired"then if not C[m]then Q[3][m]=nil;end;end;end;end end)(luraph_capture40)
				v9 = connect(heartbeat, v10)
			else
				if v9 then
					v9:Disconnect()
					v9 = nil
				end

				if type(fn27) == "function" then
					fn27()
				end
			end
		end,
	})
end

do
	local connection = nil
	local connection2 = nil
	local tbl8 = {}

	getGeneratorColor = function(arg)
		if arg.Name == "FakeGenerator" then
			return tbl.FakeGeneratorColor
		end

		if arg:FindFirstChild("Progress") and arg.Progress.Value == 100 then
			return tbl.DoneGeneratorColor
		end
		return tbl.GeneratorColor
	end

	GeneratorsEsp = function()
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if connection2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
			connection2 = nil
		end

		for _, v5 in ipairs(tbl8) do
			v5:Disconnect()
		end

		table.clear(tbl8)

		if not tbl.GeneratorEsp then
			for _, descendant in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if (descendant.Name == "Generator" or descendant.Name == "FakeGenerator") and descendant:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
					descendant.Highlight:Destroy()
				end
			end

			return
		end

		local function fn15(arg)
			if arg.Name ~= "Generator" and arg.Name ~= "FakeGenerator" then
				return
			end
			createHighlight(arg, getGeneratorColor(arg))
			local progress = arg:FindFirstChild("Progress")

			if progress then
				local connection3 = progress:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
					if tbl.GeneratorEsp and arg:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
						local v5 = getGeneratorColor(arg)
						arg.Highlight.FillColor = v5
						arg.Highlight.OutlineColor = v5
					end
				end)

				table.insert(tbl8, connection3)
			end
		end

		local function fn16(arg)
			for _, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
				fn15(child)
			end

			connection = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				task.wait(0.5)

				if tbl.GeneratorEsp and child:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
					fn15(child)
				end
			end)
		end

		local map = ingame:FindFirstChild("Map")

		if map then
			fn16(map)
		end

		connection2 = ingame.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "Map" then
				task.wait(1)

				if tbl.GeneratorEsp then
					fn16(child)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

UpdateGeneratorColors = function()
	if not tbl.GeneratorEsp then
		return
	end
	local map = ingame and ingame:FindFirstChild("Map")
	if not map then
		return
	end

	for _, child in pairs(map:GetChildren()) do
		if (child.Name == "Generator" or child.Name == "FakeGenerator") and child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
			local v5 = getGeneratorColor(child)
			child.Highlight.FillColor = v5
			child.Highlight.OutlineColor = v5
		end
	end
end

Visual:AddSection("Generators")

Visual:AddToggle({
	Title = "Generators esp",
	Callback = function(generatorEsp)
		tbl.GeneratorEsp = generatorEsp
		GeneratorsEsp()
	end,
})

local tbl8
tbl8 = {}

do
	local tbl9 = {}
	local connection = nil
	local connection2 = nil
	local connection3 = nil
	local connection4 = nil

	CreateGeneratorDrawing = function(arg)
		if tbl8[arg] then
			tbl8[arg]:Remove()
			tbl8[arg] = nil
		end

		local v5 = damnedFK.textTag()
		v5.Visible = false
		v5.Center = true
		v5.Outline = true
		v5.Font = 2
		v5.Size = tbl.EspTextSize or 14
		v5.Text = arg.Name == "FakeGenerator" and "Fake Generator" or "Generator"
		v5.Color = getGeneratorColor(arg)
		tbl8[arg] = v5
	end

	RemoveGeneratorDrawing = function(arg)
		if tbl8[arg] then
			tbl8[arg]:Remove()
			tbl8[arg] = nil
		end

		if tbl9[arg] then
			tbl9[arg] = nil
		end
	end

	local v5 = (function(Q) return function()local f=workspace.CurrentCamera;if not f then return;end;for C,m in pairs(Q[1])do if C and C.Parent then local H=Q[2][C];if not H then local d=C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Part");if d then H={MainPart=d,ProgressObj=C:FindFirstChild("Progress"),IsFake=C.Name=="FakeGenerator",Offset=Q[3](0,d.Size.Y/2+1,0),LastProgress=-1,LastColor=nil};Q[2][C]=H;else if m.Visible then m.Visible=false;end;continue;end;end;local d,E=f:WorldToViewportPoint(H.MainPart.Position+H.Offset);if E then m.Position=Q[4](d.X,d.Y);if H.IsFake then if H.LastProgress~=-2 then m.Text="Fake Generator";H.LastProgress=-2;end;else local f=H.ProgressObj;local d=f and f.Value or 0;if d~=H.LastProgress then H.LastProgress=d;m.Text=d==100 and"Generator (Done)"or(Q[5]("Generator (%d%%)",d));end;end;local Q=getGeneratorColor(C);if H.LastColor~=Q then H.LastColor=Q;m.Color=Q;end;if not m.Visible then m.Visible=true;end;elseif m.Visible then m.Visible=false;end;else RemoveGeneratorDrawing(C);end;end;end end)(luraph_capture9)

	CleanupGeneratorESP = function()
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if connection2 and not tbl.GeneratorEsp2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
			connection2 = nil
		end

		if connection3 then
			connection3:Disconnect()
			connection3 = nil
		end

		if connection4 then
			connection4:Disconnect()
			connection4 = nil
		end

		for _, v6 in pairs(tbl8) do
			v6:Remove()
		end

		table.clear(tbl8)
		table.clear(tbl9)
	end

	SetupGeneratorESP = function(arg)
		for _, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "Generator" or child.Name == "FakeGenerator" then
				CreateGeneratorDrawing(child)
			end
		end

		connection3 = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "Generator" or child.Name == "FakeGenerator" then
				task.wait(0.5)
				CreateGeneratorDrawing(child)
			end
		end)

		connection4 = arg.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "Generator" or child.Name == "FakeGenerator" then
				RemoveGeneratorDrawing(child)
			end
		end)
	end

	CheckAndSetupGeneratorESP = function()
		local map = ingame:FindFirstChild("Map")
		if map then
			SetupGeneratorESP(map)
			return true
		end
		return false
	end

	InitializeGeneratorESP = function()
		CleanupGeneratorESP()
		if not tbl.GeneratorEsp2 then
			return
		end

		if CheckAndSetupGeneratorESP() then
			connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(v5)
		end

		connection2 = ingame.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "Map" then
				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
					connection = nil
				end

				task.wait(1)
				CleanupGeneratorESP()
				SetupGeneratorESP(child)
				connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(v5)
			end
		end)
	end

	GeneratorsEsp2 = function()
		InitializeGeneratorESP()
	end

	UpdateGeneratorColors2 = function()
		for _, v6 in pairs(tbl9) do
			v6.LastColor = nil
		end
	end
end

Visual:AddToggle({
	Title = "Generators Tags",
	Callback = function(generatorEsp2)
		tbl.GeneratorEsp2 = generatorEsp2
		GeneratorsEsp2()
	end,

do

	end

		return tbl11[arg] == true
	end

	local function fn17()
		return tbl.ItemsColor or Color3.new(0.35, 0, 1)
	end

	local function fn18(arg)
		if not fn15(arg) then
			return
		end

		if not (tbl.ItemsEsp and fn16(arg.Name)) then
			return
		end

			highlight.FillColor = v5
			highlight.OutlineColor = v5
		else

		end
	end

				if tbl.ItemsEsp and fn16(descendant.Name) then
					fn18(descendant)
				else
					local highlight = descendant:FindFirstChild("Highlight")

					if highlight then
						highlight:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

		if tbl.ItemsEsp then
			for k in pairs(tbl10) do
				if fn16(k) then
					flag = true
					break
				end
			end
		end

		if flag and not connection then
			connection = ingame.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if descendant:IsA("Tool") then
					task.wait(0.1)

				end
			end)

			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil

Visual:AddSection("Objects")

do
	local connection = nil

	local tbl10 = {

			end,

				if arg.Name == "GroundBulbModel" then
					return 30
				end

				if arg.Name == "VineModel" or string.find(arg.Name, "Vine") ~= nil then
					return 20
				end
				return nil
			end,
		},
	}

	local tbl11 = {}

	for _, v5 in ipairs(tbl10) do
		tbl11[v5.Name] = true
	end

	local function fn15(arg)
		for _, v5 in ipairs(tbl10) do
			if v5.Match(arg) then
				return v5
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local tbl12 = {}
	local connection2 = nil

	local function fn16(arg)
		if arg:IsA("Model") then
			return arg.PrimaryPart or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
		end

		if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
			return arg
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function fn17(arg)
		local v5 = tbl12[arg]

		if v5 then
			v5:Remove()
			tbl12[arg] = nil
		end
	end

	local function fn18(arg, arg2, color)
		if tbl12[arg] then
			return
		end

		if not fn16(arg) then
			return
		end
		local v5 = damnedFK.textTag()
		tbl12[arg] = v5
		v5.Visible = false
		v5.Center = true
		v5.Outline = true
		v5.Font = 2
		v5.Size = tbl.EspTextSize or 14
		v5.Color = color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		v5.Text = arg2.Name
	end

	local function fn19(arg)
		local name = arg.Name or ""
		local n = arg:IsA("Model") and 2 or 1

		if name == "GroundBulbModel" or name == "VineModel" then
			n += 3
		end

		if name == "GolemStatue" or name == "MisterBeast" then
			n += 3
		end

		if string.find(name, "Model") then
			n += 1
		end

		if string.find(name, "HoverHitbox") or string.find(name, "Hitbox") then
			n -= 2
		end

		return n

	local function fn20(parent)
		if not parent or not parent.Parent then
			return
		end
		local v6 = fn15(parent)
		if not v6 then
			return
		end

		if tbl.ObjectsEsp == true and tbl11[v6.Name] == true then
			if v6.ChangeTransparency and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
				parent.Transparency = 0
			end

			local v7 = tbl[v6.Color]
			local hFillTransparency = tbl.HFillTransparency or 0.8
			local outlineTransparency = tbl.DisableOutline and 1 or 0
			local highlight = parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight")

			if not highlight then
				createHighlight(parent, v7, { CheckWorkspace = true })
				highlight = parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight")
			end

			if highlight then
				highlight.FillColor = v7
				highlight.OutlineColor = v7
				highlight.FillTransparency = hFillTransparency
				highlight.OutlineTransparency = outlineTransparency
			end
		else
			if v6.ChangeTransparency and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
				parent.Transparency = 1
			end

			local highlight = parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight")

			if highlight then
				highlight:Destroy()
			end
		end

		if tbl.ObjectsTagsEsp == true and tbl11[v6.Name] == true then
			fn18(parent, v6, tbl[v6.Color])
		else
			fn17(parent)
		end

		if v6.Radius then
			local v7 = v6.Radius(parent)
			local azureAttackRadius = parent:FindFirstChild("AzureAttackRadius")

			if v7 and tbl.AzureRadiusEsp then
				local isModel = parent:IsA("Model")
				local primaryPart

				if isModel then
					primaryPart = parent.PrimaryPart or parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
				else
					primaryPart = isModel
				end

				primaryPart = primaryPart or parent

				if primaryPart then
					if not azureAttackRadius then
						azureAttackRadius = Instance.new("CylinderHandleAdornment")
						azureAttackRadius.Name = "AzureAttackRadius"
						azureAttackRadius.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30)
						azureAttackRadius.Transparency = 0.75
						azureAttackRadius.Height = 0.05
						azureAttackRadius.AlwaysOnTop = true
						azureAttackRadius.Parent = parent
					end

					azureAttackRadius.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(primaryPart.Position) * CFrame.Angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
					azureAttackRadius.Radius = v7
					azureAttackRadius.Adornee = primaryPart
				end
			elseif azureAttackRadius then
				azureAttackRadius:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn25(arg, arg2)
		if not arg or #arg == 0 then
			return arg
		end
		arg2 = arg2 and arg2[1] or arg[1]
		local tbl14 = {}
		local tbl15 = {}
		local tbl16 = { row = arg2.row, col = arg2.col }

		for _, v8 in ipairs(arg) do
			tbl14[v8.row .. "-" .. v8.col] = { row = v8.row, col = v8.col }
		end

		table.insert(tbl15, tbl16)
		tbl14[tbl16.row .. "-" .. tbl16.col] = nil

		while next(tbl14) do
			local flag7 = false

			for k, v8 in pairs(tbl14) do
				local flag8 = v8.row == tbl16.row - 1 and v8.col == tbl16.col
				local flag9

				if flag8 then
					flag9 = flag8
				else
					flag9 = v8.row == tbl16.row + 1 and v8.col == tbl16.col
				end

				flag9 = flag9 or v8.row == tbl16.row and v8.col == tbl16.col - 1
				local flag10

				if flag9 then
					flag10 = flag9
				else
					flag10 = v8.row == tbl16.row and v8.col == tbl16.col + 1
				end

				if flag10 then
					table.insert(tbl15, v8)
					tbl14[k] = nil
					flag7 = true
					tbl16 = v8
					break
				end
			end

			if flag7 then
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		return tbl15
	end

	local function fn26(arg)
		local gridFrame = arg.gridFrame

				frame2.BackgroundTransparency = tbl12.ESPTransparency or 0.5
				frame2.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n6, n6)
				frame2.ZIndex = 7
				frame2.BackgroundColor3 = color

				frame2.Parent = frame
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn27()
		if (tbl12.EnableESP or tbl12.AutoComplete) and not flag4 then
			flag4 = true

			task.spawn(function()
				local flag7 = false

				while tbl12.EnableESP or tbl12.AutoComplete do
					task.wait(0.1)

					if FlowGameManager then
						local activeGame = FlowGameManager.activeGame

						if activeGame and activeGame.gridFrame and activeGame.gridFrame.Parent then
							if tbl12.EnableESP then
								pcall(fn26, activeGame)
							end

							if tbl12.AutoComplete and not flag7 and activeGame.Solution and activeGame.paths then
								flag7 = true

								task.spawn(function()
									for i = 1, #activeGame.Solution do
										if tbl12.AutoComplete then
											activeGame.paths[i] = {}
											local v8 = fn25(activeGame.Solution[i], activeGame.targetPairs[i])
											local solverErrorMargin = tbl12.SolverErrorMargin or 0

											if solverErrorMargin > 0 and #v8 > 2 and math.random(1, 100) <= solverErrorMargin then
												local n6 = #v8 - 1

												for i2 = 1, n6 do
													if tbl12.AutoComplete then
														table.insert(activeGame.paths[i], { row = v8[i2].row, col = v8[i2].col })

														pcall(function()
															activeGame:updateGui()
														end)

														task.wait(tbl12.ActionDelay)
														continue
													end

													break
												end

												task.wait(math.random(5, 12) / 10)

												for i2 = n6 + 1, #v8 do
													if tbl12.AutoComplete then
														table.insert(activeGame.paths[i], { row = v8[i2].row, col = v8[i2].col })

														pcall(function()
															activeGame:updateGui()
														end)

														task.wait(tbl12.ActionDelay)
														continue
													end

													break
												end
											else
												for i2 = 1, #v8 do
													if tbl12.AutoComplete then
														table.insert(activeGame.paths[i], { row = v8[i2].row, col = v8[i2].col })

														pcall(function()
															activeGame:updateGui()
														end)

														task.wait(tbl12.ActionDelay)
														continue
													end

													break
												end
											end

											continue
										end

										break
									end

									pcall(function()
										activeGame:checkForWin()
									end)

									task.wait(0.5)
									flag7 = false
								end)
							end
						else
							flag7 = false
							v7 = nil
						end
					end
				end

				flag4 = false

				if FlowGameManager and FlowGameManager.activeGame and FlowGameManager.activeGame.gridFrame and FlowGameManager.activeGame.gridFrame.Parent and FlowGameManager.activeGame.gridFrame.Parent:FindFirstChild("Generator") then
					FlowGameManager.activeGame.gridFrame.Parent.Generator:Destroy()
					v7 = nil
				end
			end)
		end
	end

					if child:FindFirstChild("Config") then
						local ok, result = pcall(require, child.Config)
						if ok and result and result.DisplayName == attribute and result.Animations and result.Animations.Block then
							animationId = result.Animations.Block
							break

					end
				end
			end
		end

			v20:Play()
		end
	end

		end

			end
		end

		return false
	end

		if not arg.Animations then
			return
		end

		if not arg._realAnims then
			arg._realAnims = {}

			for k, animation in pairs(arg.Animations) do
				arg._realAnims[k] = animation
			end
		end

		if arg3 then
			for _, v8 in ipairs(arg2) do
				if arg.Animations[v8] then
					arg.Animations[v8] = tbl18
				end
			end
		else
			for k, realAnim in pairs(arg._realAnims) do
				arg.Animations[k] = realAnim
			end
		end
	end

			return
		end

			return require(arg)
		end)

local tbl18
tbl18 = { Enabled = false, Distance = 7, Target = "Killer", Behavior = "Normal", Prediction = 15 }

local function fn19(arg)
	local v8 = Workspace.Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
	local tbl19 = {}

	if v8 then
		for _, child in ipairs(v8:GetChildren()) do
			local humanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

			if humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
				table.insert(tbl19, child)
			end
		end
	end

	return tbl19
end

getTarget = function()
	if tbl18.Target == "Yourself" then
		return localPlayer.Character
	end

	if tbl18.Target == "Killer" then
		return fn19("Killers")[1]
	end
	local Survivors2 = fn19("Survivors")
	if tbl18.Target == "Survivor" then
		return Survivors2[1]
	end

	if tbl18.Target == "Nearest Survivor" then
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			return nil
		end
		local position = character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
		local huge = math.huge
		local v8 = nil

		for _, v9 in ipairs(Survivors2) do
			if v9 ~= character then
				local magnitude = (v9.HumanoidRootPart.Position - position).Magnitude

				if magnitude < huge then
					huge = magnitude
					v8 = v9
				end
			end
		end

		return v8
	end

	if tbl18.Target == "Lowest HP" then
		local v8, v9, v10 = ipairs(Survivors2)
		local huge = math.huge
		local v11 = nil

		for _, v12 in v8, v9, v10 do
			local health = v12.Humanoid.Health

			if health < huge then
				huge = health
				v11 = v12
			end
		end

		return v11
	end

	return nil
end

calculateBehaviorPosition = function(arg)
	local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not humanoidRootPart then
		return nil
	end
	local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local unit = assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 1 and assemblyLinearVelocity.Unit or humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
	local n = humanoidRootPart.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * tbl18.Prediction / 100
	local distance = tbl18.Distance
	local now = tick()
	if tbl18.Behavior == "Normal" then
		return n
	end

	if tbl18.Behavior == "Front" then
		return n + unit * distance
	end

	if tbl18.Behavior == "Back" then
		return n - unit * distance
	end

	if tbl18.Behavior == "Orbit" then
		return n + Vector3.new(math.sin(now * 4) * distance, 0, math.cos(now * 4) * distance)
	end

	if tbl18.Behavior == "Strafe" then
		return n + unit * distance + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.RightVector * math.sin(now * 6) * distance / 1.5
	end

	if tbl18.Behavior == "Run Away" then
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			local humanoidRootPart2 = character.HumanoidRootPart
			return humanoidRootPart2.Position + humanoidRootPart2.CFrame.LookVector * distance * 1.5
		end
		return n
	end

	return n
end

	local function fn26(arg)
		if type(arg) ~= "table" then
			return
		end
		local sk8 = arg.Sk8
		if type(sk8) ~= "table" or sk8.__bypassed then
			return
		end
		local callback = sk8.Callback
		if type(callback) ~= "function" then
			return
		end
		sk8.__origCallback = callback
		sk8.__bypassed = true

		sk8.Callback = function(arg2, arg3)
			if arg3 == "BAD" then
				arg3 = nil
			end

			return callback(arg2, arg3)
		end
	end

	local function fn27(arg)
		if type(arg) ~= "table" then
			return
		end
		local sk8 = arg.Sk8
		if type(sk8) ~= "table" or not sk8.__bypassed then
			return
		end

		if sk8.__origCallback then
			sk8.Callback = sk8.__origCallback
			sk8.__origCallback = nil
		end

		sk8.__bypassed = nil
	end

	local function fn28()
		if not Util or type(Util.GetActor) ~= "function" then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(Util.GetActor, Util, localPlayer)
		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			return result
		end
		return nil
	end

	AutoPhase_FUNC = function()
		if thread2 then
			task.cancel(thread2)

		local Network = nil

									end

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end

	local function fn20(arg)
		local mainUI = playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
		mainUI = mainUI and mainUI:FindFirstChild("AbilityContainer")
		mainUI = mainUI and mainUI:FindFirstChild(arg)
		if not mainUI then

		local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

			return false
		end

			local animation = v8.Animation

				animation = string.find(tostring(v8.Animation.AnimationId), targetAnimID)
			end

			if animation then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn22(arg, arg2)
		local magnitude = (arg.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude
		if tbl19.RangeMode == "Around" then
			return magnitude <= tbl19.MaxDist
		end
		local n = arg.Position - arg2.Position
		local v8 = n:Dot(arg2.CFrame.LookVector)
		local n7 = n.Magnitude > 0 and n.Unit:Dot(arg2.CFrame.LookVector) or 0
		return (tbl19.DirectBehind and n7 <= -0.85 or v8 < 0.3) and magnitude <= tbl19.MaxDist
	end

	local v8 = (function(Q) return function()if not Q[1]then return;end;local f=os.clock();for C,m in pairs(Q[1]:GetChildren())do C=m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if C then local H,d,E=Q[2][6][Q[2][3]][C],C.Position,C.CFrame.LookVector;if H and H.t and H.pos then m=f-H.t;if m>0 then local L=(d-H.pos)/m;local a=H.vel and(H.vel:Lerp(L,math.clamp(m*10,0,1)))or L;Q[2][6][Q[2][3]][C]={pos=d,look=E,vel=a,t=f,prevLook=H.look};else Q[2][6][Q[2][3]][C]={pos=d,look=E,vel=Vector3.new(0,0,0),t=f,prevLook=H and H.look or E};end;else Q[2][6][Q[2][3]][C]={pos=d,look=E,vel=Vector3.new(0,0,0),t=f,prevLook=E};end;end;end;end end)(luraph_capture17)

	local function fn23(arg, arg2)
		local cFrame = arg2.CFrame
		local n = cFrame.Position - cFrame.LookVector.Unit * tbl19.BehindDist
		local vector2 = Vector3.new(n.X, cFrame.Position.Y, n.Z)
		if not tbl19.Prediction then
			return CFrame.new(vector2, vector2 + cFrame.LookVector.Unit)
		end
		local v9 = tbl20[arg2]
		local vel = v9 and v9.vel or arg2.Velocity or Vector3.zero
		local n7 = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z) * tbl19.PredTime * tbl19.PredStrength
		local lookVector = cFrame.LookVector
		local rightVector = cFrame.RightVector
		local n8 = lookVector * n7:Dot(lookVector)
		local n9 = rightVector * n7:Dot(rightVector)
		local prevLook = v9 and v9.prevLook
		local vector3 = Vector3.zero

		if prevLook then
			local n10 = lookVector - v9.prevLook
			local predStrength = tbl19.PredStrength
			vector3 = rightVector * n10:Dot(rightVector) * 3 * predStrength
		end

		local n10 = n8 + n9 + vector3
		local n11 = vector2 + Vector3.new(n10.X, 0, n10.Z)
		return CFrame.new(n11, n11 + cFrame.LookVector.Unit)
	end

	end

	local v9 = (function(Q) return function()if Q[1][6][Q[1][3]]then return;end;local f=Q[2].Character;local C=f and(f:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"));if not C then return;end;local m=nil;local H=Q[3].MaxDist;if Q[4]then for d,E in ipairs(Q[4]:GetChildren())do local L,a=E:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),E:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid");if L and a and a.Health>0 and(Q[5][6][Q[5][3]](C,L))then d=(C.Position-L.Position).Magnitude;if d<=H then H,m=d,E;end;end;end;end;if not m then return;end;H=m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if m~=f then for d,d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants())do if d:IsA("BasePart")or(d:IsA("MeshPart"))then d.CanCollide=not Q[3].KillerNoclip;end;end;end;if Q[3].GlideStab and(Q[6][6][Q[6][3]](f))then if Q[3].GlideStabType=="Teleport"then C.CFrame=Q[7][6][Q[7][3]](C,H);elseif Q[3].AimingMode=="Character"then C.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(C.Position,H.Position);elseif Q[3].AimingMode=="Camera"and Q[8]then Q[8].CFrame=CFrame.new(Q[8].CFrame.Position,H.Position);end;return;end;if Q[3].Enabled and(Q[9][6][Q[9][3]]("Dagger"))then if os.clock()-Q[10][6][Q[10][3]]<5 then return;end;Q[10][6][Q[10][3]]=os.clock();Q[11][6][Q[11][3]](f,C,H);end;end end)(luraph_capture18)

do

		end

		end

			local humanoid = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

							if tbl.Shoot_360_Spin then

									tick()

									local v8 = (function(Q) return function()if not Q[1]or not Q[1].Parent then Q[2][6][Q[2][3]]:Disconnect();return;end;local f=tick()-Q[3];if f<=Q[4]then local C=f/Q[4];local f=math.rad(360*C);Q[1].CFrame=CFrame.new(Q[1].Position)*CFrame.Angles(0,f,0);else Q[2][6][Q[2][3]]:Disconnect();end;end end)(luraph_capture56)

		end
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local v8 = tbl20[character.Name]

		if v8 and remoteEvent then
			remoteEvent:FireServer("UseActorAbility", { buffer.fromstring(v8) })
			n = tick()
		end
	end

		local match = tostring(arg.SoundId):match("%d+")

		if match and tbl2.TriggerSounds[match] then
			if fn13({ Folder = survivors, MaxDist = tbl2.ChanceSounds[match] and 75 or tbl19.Radius, IgnoreSelf = true }) then
				fn21()
			end

				local v15 = state.VelocityTable[child]

					else

					end

				end
			end
		end

			if not tbl22[k] then
				state.VelocityTable[k] = nil
			end
		end
	end

					huge = magnitude
					v15 = child
				end
			end
		end

		return v15
	end

	local function fn37(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if arg3 <= 0 then
			return 0
		end
		local n11 = arg2:Dot(arg2) - arg3 * arg3
		local n12 = 2 * arg:Dot(arg2)
		local v14 = arg:Dot(arg)
		if v14 < 0.0001 then
			return 0
		end

		if math.abs(n11) < 0.0001 then
			if math.abs(n12) > 0.0001 and -v14 / n12 >= 0 then
				return -v14 / n12
			end
		else
			local n13 = n12 * n12 - 4 * n11 * v14

			if n13 >= 0 then
				local v15 = math.sqrt(n13)
				local n14 = (-n12 - v15) / 2 * n11
				local n15 = (-n12 + v15) / 2 * n11
				local huge = math.huge

				if not (n14 >= 0) then
					n14 = huge
				end

				local n16

				if not (n15 >= 0) then
					n16 = n14
				else
					n16 = math.min(n14, n15)
				end

				if n16 < math.huge then
					return n16
				end
			end
		end

		return arg.Magnitude / arg3
	end

	local function fn38(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		local velocity = state.VelocityTable[arg]
		velocity = velocity and velocity.Velocity or arg2.AssemblyLinearVelocity

		if velocity.Magnitude > settings.MaxTargetSpeed then
			velocity = Vector3.zero
		end

		if arg5 == "Grab" then
			hookSpeed = arg4.Config.Grab.HookSpeed or 270
			additionalLeadTime = settings.AdditionalLeadTime
		else

			hookSpeed = (golemFissure.StudInterval or 5) / math.max(0.001, golemFissure.TimeInterval or 0.07)
			additionalLeadTime = math.max(0, state.AimEndTime - os.clock()) + settings.AdditionalLeadTime
			velocity *= Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
		end

		return arg2.Position + velocity * math.clamp(additionalLeadTime + fn37(arg2.Position + velocity * additionalLeadTime - arg3, velocity, hookSpeed), 0, settings.MaxPredictTime)
	end

local connection3 = nil

Auto_Nosferatu_Hook_FUNC = function()
	if connection3 then
		connection3:Disconnect()
		connection3 = nil
	end

	if not tbl.ANosferatuHook then
		return
	end
	local temporaryUI = localPlayer.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TemporaryUI", 5)
	if not temporaryUI then
		return
	end

	local function fn21(arg)
		task.spawn(function()
			while tbl.ANosferatuHook do
				if not (not arg or not arg:IsDescendantOf(game)) then
					local killers2 = workspace.Players:FindFirstChild("Killers")
					killers2 = killers2 and killers2:FindFirstChild("Nosferatu")
					local playerFromCharacter = killers2 and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(killers2)

					if playerFromCharacter and remoteEvent then
						remoteEvent:FireServer(playerFromCharacter.Name .. "NosHookQTE", { buffer.fromstring("\1\1") })
					end

					task.wait(tbl.ANosferatuHookCoolDown)
					continue
				end

				break
			end
		end)
	end

	local qte = temporaryUI:FindFirstChild("QTE")

	if qte and qte:IsA("Frame") then
		fn21(qte)
	end

	connection3 = temporaryUI.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "QTE" then
			task.wait(1)
			fn21(child)
		end
	end)
end

		end

		if v15 < 0.001 then

		end

	end

	end)

	local tbl21 = {}
	local tbl22 = {}

	local function fn21(arg)
		if statusEffects:FindFirstChild(arg) then
			return statusEffects[arg]
		end

		for _, v14 in pairs({ "KillerExclusive", "SurvivorExclusive" }) do
			local v15 = statusEffects:FindFirstChild(v14)
			if v15 and v15:FindFirstChild(arg) then
				return v15[arg]
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn22(arg)
		local v14 = fn21(arg)
		if not v14 or tbl22[v14] then
			return
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(require, v14)

		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			for k, v15 in pairs(result) do
				if type(v15) == "function" then
					result[k] = function(...)
						if tbl21[arg] then
							return
						end
						return v15(...)
					end
				end
			end

			tbl22[v14] = true
		end
	end

							character:SetAttribute("NoFallSlow", true)
						end
					end

			end)

			local character = localPlayer.Character

		end
	end

	local function fn32(arg)
		if connection12 then
			connection12:Disconnect()
			connection12 = nil
		end

		if connection13 then
			connection13:Disconnect()
			connection13 = nil
		end

		local function fn33(arg2)
			if not arg2 then
				return
			end
			local mapBoundaries = arg2:FindFirstChild("MapBoundaries")
			if not mapBoundaries then
				return
			end

			local function fn34(arg3)
				if not arg3 then
					return

			local killerDoors = mapBoundaries:FindFirstChild("KillerDoors")

			if not arg then
				fn34(killerDoors)
				fn34(killerOnly)
				return

			fn34(killerOnly)

				if isBasePart then
					isBasePart = descendant:IsDescendantOf(killerDoors or descendant)

					if not isBasePart then
						isBasePart = descendant:IsDescendantOf(killerOnly or descendant)
					end
				end

				if isBasePart then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end)
		end

		end

		end
	end

Misc:AddSection("Lighting")

do
	local color2 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
	local color3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
	local connection4 = nil
	local connection5 = nil

	local function fn21()
		if Lighting.FogEnd ~= 9999 then
			Lighting.FogEnd = 9999
		end

		if Lighting.GlobalShadows ~= false then
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
		end

		if Lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= color2 then
			Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = color2
		end

		if Lighting.Ambient ~= color3 then
			Lighting.Ambient = color3
		end

		local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")

		if atmosphere then
			atmosphere:Destroy()
		end
	end

	Misc:AddToggle({
		Title = "Full Bright + No Fog",
		Callback = function(arg)
			if arg then
				fn21()
				connection4 = Lighting.Changed:Connect(fn21)
				connection5 = Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(fn21)
			else
				if connection4 then
					connection4:Disconnect()
					connection4 = nil
				end

				if connection5 then
					connection5:Disconnect()
					connection5 = nil
				end
			end
		end,
	})
end

Misc:AddSection("Chat")

do
	local chatWindowConfiguration = nil
	local connection4 = nil
	local connection5 = nil

	local function fn21()
		if not chatWindowConfiguration then
			chatWindowConfiguration = TextChatService:FindFirstChildOfClass("ChatWindowConfiguration")
		end

		if chatWindowConfiguration and chatWindowConfiguration.Enabled ~= true then
			chatWindowConfiguration.Enabled = true
		end
	end

	Misc:AddToggle({
		Title = "Turn on chat",
		Callback = function(arg)
			if arg then
				fn21()

				if chatWindowConfiguration then
					connection4 = chatWindowConfiguration:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(fn21)
				end

				connection5 = TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					if child:IsA("ChatWindowConfiguration") then
						chatWindowConfiguration = child
						fn21()

						if connection4 then
							connection4:Disconnect()
						end

						connection4 = chatWindowConfiguration:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(fn21)
					end
				end)
			else
				if connection4 then
					connection4:Disconnect()
					connection4 = nil
				end

				if connection5 then
					connection5:Disconnect()
					connection5 = nil
				end
			end
		end,
	})
end

do
	local flag4 = false

	local function fn21()
		flag4 = tbl.SidebarVisibility
		local sidebarHandler = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Systems") and ReplicatedStorage.Systems:FindFirstChild("Player") and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player:FindFirstChild("UI") and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player.UI:FindFirstChild("SidebarHandler")
		local v14 = nil

		if sidebarHandler then
			local ok, result = pcall(require, sidebarHandler)

			if ok and type(result) == "table" then
				v14 = result
			end
		end

		if not flag4 then
			if v14 then
				pcall(function()
					v14:ToggleSidebarButtons(false)
				end)
			end

			local mainUI = playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
			local sidebar = mainUI and mainUI:FindFirstChild("Sidebar")

			if sidebar then
				sidebar.Visible = false
			end

			return
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while flag4 and tbl.SidebarVisibility do
				if v14 then
					pcall(function()
						v14:ToggleSidebarButtons(true)
					end)
				end

				local mainUI = playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
				mainUI = mainUI and mainUI:FindFirstChild("Sidebar")

				if mainUI and mainUI.Visible ~= true then
					mainUI.Visible = true
				end

				task.wait(0.2)
			end
		end)
	end

	Misc:AddToggle({
		Title = "Enable Sidebar Visibility",
		Callback = function(sidebarVisibility)
			tbl.SidebarVisibility = sidebarVisibility
			fn21()
		end,
	})
end

Misc:AddSection("Camera")

Misc:AddToggle({
	Title = "Infinite Zoom + Noclip",
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 10000
		else
			localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 12
		end

		local setconstant_ = debug and debug.setconstant or setconstant
		local getconstants_ = debug and debug.getconstants or getconstants
		local popper = localPlayer.PlayerScripts.PlayerModule.CameraModule.ZoomController.Popper

		for _, v14 in pairs(getgc()) do
			if type(v14) == "function" and getfenv(v14).script == popper then
				for k, v15 in pairs(getconstants_(v14)) do
					local num = tonumber(v15)

					if arg and num == 0.25 then
						setconstant_(v14, k, 0)
					elseif not arg and num == 0 then
						setconstant_(v14, k, 0.25)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end,
})

					end

							local ok, result = pcall(function()

								animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://136252471123500"

	local function fn31(arg, arg2, arg3)

		end

		end

		if tbl21.ActiveTrack then
			pcall(function()
				tbl21.ActiveTrack:Stop()
			end)
		end

		if lastKey == "Stunned" then
			if tbl21.LoadedAnims.StunnedLoop then
				tbl21.ActiveTrack = tbl21.LoadedAnims.StunnedLoop
				tbl21.ActiveTrack:Play()
				tbl21.LastKey = lastKey
			end

			return
		end

		if tbl21.LoadedAnims[lastKey] then
			tbl21.ActiveTrack = tbl21.LoadedAnims[lastKey]
			tbl21.ActiveTrack:Play()
			tbl21.LastKey = lastKey
		end
	end

	local function fn31()
		fn28()
		if not tbl.UseKillerAnimations then
			return
		end
		local v19 = fn25()
		if not v19 then
			return
		end
		local humanoid = (localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		fn29(v19, humanoid)
		local v20 = tbl21
		local renderStepped = RunService.RenderStepped
		local connect = renderStepped.Connect
		local v21 = (function(Q) return function()if not Q[1].UseKillerAnimations or Q[2].Health<=0 then return;end;Q[3][6][Q[3][3]](Q[4][6][Q[4][3]](Q[2],Q[5]));end end)(luraph_capture80)
		v20.AnimUpdateConn = connect(renderStepped, v21)
	end

	local function fn32()
		for _, playingSound in ipairs(tbl21.PlayingSounds) do
			pcall(function()
				playingSound:Stop()
				playingSound:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		tbl21.PlayingSounds = {}
	end

	local function fn33()
		fn32()
		if not tbl.UseKillerSounds then
			return
		end
		local v19 = fn25()
		if not v19 or not v19.Sounds then
			return
		end

		for _, sound in pairs(v19.Sounds) do
			if typeof(sound) == "string" then
				local sound2 = Instance.new("Sound")
				sound2.Parent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
				sound2.SoundId = sound
				sound2.Volume = 1
				sound2:Play()
				table.insert(tbl21.PlayingSounds, sound2)
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn34()
		if not tbl.SelectedKiller or not tbl.UseKillerAnimations then
			return
		end
		local v19 = fn25()
		if not v19 or not v19.Animations then
			return
		end
		local attack = v19.Animations.Attack or v19.Animations.Slash or v19.Animations.Stab
		if not attack then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			return
		end
		local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChild("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)

		if tbl21.AttackTrack then
			tbl21.AttackTrack:Stop()
			tbl21.AttackTrack = nil
		end

		local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
		animation.AnimationId = type(attack) == "table" and attack[1] or attack
		tbl21.AttackTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)

		if tbl21.AttackTrack then
			tbl21.AttackTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
			tbl21.AttackTrack:Play()
			local length = tbl21.AttackTrack.Length

			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(length > 0 and length or 1)

				if tbl21.AttackTrack then
					tbl21.AttackTrack:Stop()
					tbl21.AttackTrack = nil
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function fn35()
		if tbl21.MobileAttackCreated then
			return
		end

		local v19 = fn7({
			name = "MobileAttack",
			text = "ATTACK",
			position = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.5, 0),
			backgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0),
			borderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
			mobileOnly = true,
			onClick = function()
				fn34()
			end,
			onClose = function()
				tbl21.MobileAttackCreated = false
				tbl21.MobileAttackBtn = nil
				tbl21.MobileAttackClose = nil
				tbl.MobileAttackButtonEnabled = false
			end,
		})

		if v19 then
			tbl21.MobileAttackGui = v19.gui
			tbl21.MobileAttackBtn = v19.button
			tbl21.MobileAttackClose = v19.closeButton
			tbl21.MobileAttackCreated = true
		end
	end

	Animations:AddButton({
		Name = "Load Killer Animations",
		DestroyOnClick = true,
		Callback = function()
			Animations:AddDropdown({
				Name = "Select Killer",
				Options = fn24(),
				Callback = function(selectedKiller)
					tbl.SelectedKiller = selectedKiller
					tbl.SelectedSkin = "Default"
					local tbl22 = { "Default" }

					if killers3 then
						local v19 = killers3:FindFirstChild(selectedKiller)

						if v19 then
							for _, child in ipairs(v19:GetChildren()) do
								if child:FindFirstChild("Config") then
									local ok, result = pcall(require, child.Config)

									if ok and type(result) == "table" and result.Animations then
										table.insert(tbl22, child.Name)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if v18 then
						v18:UpdateOptions(tbl22)
						v18:Set("Default")
					end

					if tbl.UseKillerAnimations then
						fn31()
					end

					if tbl.UseKillerSounds then
						fn33()
					end
				end,
			})

			v18 = Animations:AddDropdown({
				Name = "Select Skin",
				Options = { "Default" },
				Default = "Default",
				Callback = function(selectedSkin)
					tbl.SelectedSkin = selectedSkin

					if tbl.UseKillerAnimations then
						fn31()
					end

					if tbl.UseKillerSounds then
						fn33()
					end
				end,
			})

			Animations:AddToggle({
				Title = "Replace Animations",
				Callback = function(useKillerAnimations)
					tbl.UseKillerAnimations = useKillerAnimations

					if useKillerAnimations then
						fn31()
					else
						fn28()
					end
				end,
			})

			Animations:AddToggle({
				Title = "Replace Sounds",
				Callback = function(useKillerSounds)
					tbl.UseKillerSounds = useKillerSounds

					if useKillerSounds then
						fn33()
					else
						fn32()
					end
				end,
			})

			Animations:AddKeybind({
				Title = "Hit animation",
				Default = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
				Callback = function()
					fn34()
				end,
			})

			Animations:AddToggle({
				Title = "Mobile attack button",
				Callback = function(mobileAttackButtonEnabled)
					tbl.MobileAttackButtonEnabled = mobileAttackButtonEnabled

					if mobileAttackButtonEnabled then
						if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
							fn35()
						else
							tbl.MobileAttackButtonEnabled = false
						end
					else
						fn8("MobileAttack")
						tbl21.MobileAttackCreated = false
					end
				end,
			})
		end,
	})
end

task.wait()

do
	local Music = v2:AddTab("Music")

	local themes = workspace:FindFirstChild("Themes")

	local function fn21(arg)
		return tostring(arg):match("%d+") or ""
	end

	local tbl21 = {
